     * <p><b>204</b> - 
     * @param version  (required)
     * @param categorySlug  (required)
     * @throws RestClientException if an error occurs while attempting to invoke the API
     */
    public void apiSettingsDelete(String version, String categorySlug) throws RestClientException {
        apiSettingsDeleteWithHttpInfo(version, categorySlug);
    }

(choice)     - &#x60;None&#x60;: ---------     - &#x60;logstash&#x60;     - &#x60;splunk&#x60;     - &#x60;loggly&#x60;     - &#x60;sumologic&#x60;     - &#x60;other&#x60; * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_USERNAME&#x60;: Username for external log aggregator (if required; HTTP/s only). (string) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_PASSWORD&#x60;: Password or authentication token for external log aggregator (if required; HTTP/s only). (string) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_LOGGERS&#x60;: List of loggers that will send HTTP logs to the collector, these can include any or all of:  awx - service logs activity_stream - activity stream records job_events - callback data from Ansible job events system_tracking - facts gathered from scan jobs broadcast_websocket - errors pertaining to websockets broadcast metrics job_lifecycle - logs related to processing of a job  (list) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_INDIVIDUAL_FACTS&#x60;: If set, system tracking facts will be sent for each package, service, or other item found in a scan, allowing for greater search query granularity. If unset, facts will be sent as a single dictionary, allowing for greater efficiency in fact processing. (boolean) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_ENABLED&#x60;: Enable sending logs to external log aggregator. (boolean) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_TOWER_UUID&#x60;: Useful to uniquely identify instances. (string) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_PROTOCOL&#x60;: Protocol used to communicate with log aggregator.  HTTPS/HTTP assumes HTTPS unless http:// is explicitly used in the Logging Aggregator hostname. (choice)     - &#x60;https&#x60;: HTTPS/HTTP     - &#x60;tcp&#x60;: TCP     - &#x60;udp&#x60;: UDP * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_TCP_TIMEOUT&#x60;: Number of seconds for a TCP connection to external log aggregator to timeout. Applies to HTTPS and TCP log aggregator protocols. (integer) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_VERIFY_CERT&#x60;: Flag to control enable/disable of certificate verification when LOG_AGGREGATOR_PROTOCOL is &amp;quot;https&amp;quot;. If enabled, the log handler will verify certificate sent by external log aggregator before establishing connection. (boolean) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_LEVEL&#x60;: Level threshold used by log handler. Severities from lowest to highest are DEBUG, INFO, WARNING, ERROR, CRITICAL. Messages less severe than the threshold will be ignored by log handler. (messages under category awx.anlytics ignore this setting) (choice)     - &#x60;DEBUG&#x60;     - &#x60;INFO&#x60;     - &#x60;WARNING&#x60;     - &#x60;ERROR&#x60;     - &#x60;CRITICAL&#x60; * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_ACTION_QUEUE_SIZE&#x60;: Defines how large the rsyslog action queue can grow in number of messages stored. This can have an impact on memory utilization. When the queue reaches 75% of this number, the queue will start writing to disk (queue.highWatermark in rsyslog). When it reaches 90%, NOTICE, INFO, and DEBUG messages will start to be discarded (queue.discardMark with queue.discardSeverity&#x3D;5). (integer) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_ACTION_MAX_DISK_USAGE_GB&#x60;: Amount of data to store (in gigabytes) if an rsyslog action takes time to process an incoming message (defaults to 1). Equivalent to the rsyslogd queue.maxdiskspace setting on the action (e.g. omhttp). It stores files in the directory specified by LOG_AGGREGATOR_MAX_DISK_USAGE_PATH. (integer) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_MAX_DISK_USAGE_PATH&#x60;: Location to persist logs that should be retried after an outage of the external log aggregator (defaults to /var/lib/awx). Equivalent to the rsyslogd queue.spoolDirectory setting. (string) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_RSYSLOGD_DEBUG&#x60;: Enabled high verbosity debugging for rsyslogd.  Useful for debugging connection issues for external log aggregation. (boolean) * &#x60;API_400_ERROR_LOG_FORMAT&#x60;: The format of logged messages when an API 4XX error occurs, the following variables will be substituted:  status_code - The HTTP status code of the error user_name - The user name attempting to use the API url_path - The URL path to the API endpoint called remote_addr - The remote address seen for the user error - The error set by the api endpoint Variables need to be in the format {&amp;lt;variable name&amp;gt;}. (integer) * &#x60;IS_K8S&#x60;: Indicates whether the instance is part of a kubernetes-based deployment. Use a value of 0 to indicate that no timeout should be imposed. (integer) * &#x60;MAX_FORKS&#x60;: Saving a Job Template with more than this number of forks will result in an error. (string) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_LOGGERS&#x60;: List of loggers that will send HTTP logs to the collector, these can include any or all of:  awx - service logs activity_stream - activity stream records job_events - callback data from Ansible job events system_tracking - facts gathered from scan jobs broadcast_websocket - errors pertaining to websockets broadcast metrics job_lifecycle - logs related to processing of a job  (list) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_INDIVIDUAL_FACTS&#x60;: If set, system tracking facts will be sent for each package, service, or other item found in a scan, allowing for greater search query granularity. If unset, facts will be sent as a single dictionary, allowing for greater efficiency in fact processing. (boolean) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_ENABLED&#x60;: Enable sending logs to external log aggregator. (boolean) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_TOWER_UUID&#x60;: Useful to uniquely identify instances. (string) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_PROTOCOL&#x60;: Protocol used to communicate with log aggregator.  HTTPS/HTTP assumes HTTPS unless http:// is explicitly used in the Logging Aggregator hostname. Applies to HTTPS and TCP log aggregator protocols. (messages under category awx.anlytics ignore this setting) (choice)     - &#x60;DEBUG&#x60;     - &#x60;INFO&#x60;     - &#x60;WARNING&#x60;     - &#x60;ERROR&#x60;     - &#x60;CRITICAL&#x60; * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_ACTION_QUEUE_SIZE&#x60;: Defines how large the rsyslog action queue can grow in number of messages stored. This can have an impact on memory utilization. When the queue reaches 75% of this number, the queue will start writing to disk (queue.highWatermark in rsyslog). When it reaches 90%, NOTICE, INFO, and DEBUG messages will start to be discarded (queue.discardMark with queue.discardSeverity&#x3D;5). (integer) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_ACTION_MAX_DISK_USAGE_GB&#x60;: Amount of data to store (in gigabytes) if an rsyslog action takes time to process an incoming message (defaults to 1). (integer) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_MAX_DISK_USAGE_PATH&#x60;: Location to persist logs that should be retried after an outage of the external log aggregator (defaults to /var/lib/awx). (string, default&#x3D;&#x60;\&quot;status {status_code} received by user {user_name} attempting to access {url_path} from {remote_addr}\&quot;&#x60;) * &#x60;AUTOMATION_ANALYTICS_LAST_GATHER&#x60;:  (datetime, default&#x3D;&#x60;None&#x60;) * &#x60;AUTOMATION_ANALYTICS_LAST_ENTRIES&#x60;:  (string, default&#x3D;&#x60;\&quot;\&quot;&#x60;) * &#x60;AUTOMATION_ANALYTICS_GATHER_INTERVAL&#x60;: Interval (in seconds) between data gathering. (integer, default&#x3D;&#x60;14400&#x60;)  * &#x60;BULK_JOB_MAX_LAUNCH&#x60;: Max jobs to allow bulk jobs to launch (integer, default&#x3D;&#x60;100&#x60;) * &#x60;BULK_HOST_MAX_CREATE&#x60;: Max number of hosts to allow to be created in a single bulk action (integer, default&#x3D;&#x60;100&#x60;) * &#x60;BULK_HOST_MAX_DELETE&#x60;: Max number of hosts to allow to be deleted in a single bulk action (integer, default&#x3D;&#x60;250&#x60;) * &#x60;SUBSCRIPTION_USAGE_MODEL&#x60;:  (choice)     - &#x60;\&quot;\&quot;&#x60;: No subscription. (integer, default&#x3D;&#x60;-1&#x60;) * &#x60;DISABLE_LOCAL_AUTH&#x60;: Controls whether users are prevented from using the built-in authentication system.  (boolean, default&#x3D;&#x60;False&#x60;) * &#x60;AUTH_BASIC_ENABLED&#x60;: Enable HTTP Basic Auth for the API Browser. (boolean, default&#x3D;&#x60;True&#x60;) * &#x60;LOGIN_REDIRECT_OVERRIDE&#x60;: URL to which unauthorized users will be redirected to log in.  If blank, users will be sent to the login page. (string, default&#x3D;&#x60;\&quot;\&quot;&#x60;) * &#x60;ALLOW_METRICS_FOR_ANONYMOUS_USERS&#x60;: If true, anonymous users are allowed to poll metrics. (boolean, default&#x3D;&#x60;False&#x60;)  * &#x60;CUSTOM_LOGIN_INFO&#x60;: If needed, you can add specific information (such as a legal notice or a disclaimer) to a text box in the login modal using this setting. (string, default&#x3D;&#x60;\&quot;\&quot;&#x60;) * &#x60;CUSTOM_LOGO&#x60;: To set up a custom logo, provide a file that you create. (string, default&#x3D;&#x60;\&quot;\&quot;&#x60;) * &#x60;MAX_UI_JOB_EVENTS&#x60;: Maximum number of job events for the UI to retrieve within a single request. (integer, default&#x3D;&#x60;4000&#x60;) * &#x60;UI_LIVE_UPDATES_ENABLED&#x60;: If disabled, the page will not refresh when events are received. Reloading the page will be required to get the latest details. (boolean, default&#x3D;&#x60;True&#x60;)         For a PUT request, include **all** fields in the request.    For a PATCH request, include only the fields that are being modified.    # Delete a Setting:  Make a DELETE request to this resource to delete this setting.
     * <p><b>200</b> - 
     * <p><b>400</b>
     * @param version  (required)
     * @param categorySlug  (required)
     * @param data  (optional)
     * @return SettingSingleton
     * @throws RestClientException if an error occurs while attempting to invoke the API
     */
    public SettingSingleton apiSettingsPartialUpdate(String version, String categorySlug, Object data) throws RestClientException {
        return apiSettingsPartialUpdateWithHttpInfo(version, categorySlug, data).getBody();
    }

    public ResponseEntity<SettingSingleton> apiSettingsPartialUpdateWithHttpInfo(String version, String categorySlug, Object data) throws RestClientException {

     * <p><b>200</b> - 
     * @param version  (required)
     * @param categorySlug  (required)
     * @return SettingSingleton
     * @throws RestClientException if an error occurs while attempting to invoke the API
     */
    public SettingSingleton apiSettingsRead(String version, String categorySlug) throws RestClientException {
        return apiSettingsReadWithHttpInfo(version, categorySlug).getBody();
    }

(choice)     - &#x60;None&#x60;: ---------     - &#x60;logstash&#x60;     - &#x60;splunk&#x60;     - &#x60;loggly&#x60;     - &#x60;sumologic&#x60;     - &#x60;other&#x60; * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_USERNAME&#x60;: Username for external log aggregator (if required; HTTP/s only). (string) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_PASSWORD&#x60;: Password or authentication token for external log aggregator (if required; HTTP/s only). (string) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_LOGGERS&#x60;: List of loggers that will send HTTP logs to the collector, these can include any or all of:  awx - service logs activity_stream - activity stream records job_events - callback data from Ansible job events system_tracking - facts gathered from scan jobs broadcast_websocket - errors pertaining to websockets broadcast metrics job_lifecycle - logs related to processing of a job  (list) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_INDIVIDUAL_FACTS&#x60;: If set, system tracking facts will be sent for each package, service, or other item found in a scan, allowing for greater search query granularity. If unset, facts will be sent as a single dictionary, allowing for greater efficiency in fact processing. (boolean) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_ENABLED&#x60;: Enable sending logs to external log aggregator. (boolean) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_TOWER_UUID&#x60;: Useful to uniquely identify instances. (string) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_PROTOCOL&#x60;: Protocol used to communicate with log aggregator.  HTTPS/HTTP assumes HTTPS unless http:// is explicitly used in the Logging Aggregator hostname. (choice)     - &#x60;https&#x60;: HTTPS/HTTP     - &#x60;tcp&#x60;: TCP     - &#x60;udp&#x60;: UDP * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_TCP_TIMEOUT&#x60;: Number of seconds for a TCP connection to external log aggregator to timeout. Applies to HTTPS and TCP log aggregator protocols. (integer) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_VERIFY_CERT&#x60;: Flag to control enable/disable of certificate verification when LOG_AGGREGATOR_PROTOCOL is &amp;quot;https&amp;quot;. If enabled, the log handler will verify certificate sent by external log aggregator before establishing connection. (boolean) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_LEVEL&#x60;: Level threshold used by log handler. Severities from lowest to highest are DEBUG, INFO, WARNING, ERROR, CRITICAL. Messages less severe than the threshold will be ignored by log handler. (messages under category awx.anlytics ignore this setting) (choice)     - &#x60;DEBUG&#x60;     - &#x60;INFO&#x60;     - &#x60;WARNING&#x60;     - &#x60;ERROR&#x60;     - &#x60;CRITICAL&#x60; * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_ACTION_QUEUE_SIZE&#x60;: Defines how large the rsyslog action queue can grow in number of messages stored. This can have an impact on memory utilization. When the queue reaches 75% of this number, the queue will start writing to disk (queue.highWatermark in rsyslog). When it reaches 90%, NOTICE, INFO, and DEBUG messages will start to be discarded (queue.discardMark with queue.discardSeverity&#x3D;5). (integer) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_ACTION_MAX_DISK_USAGE_GB&#x60;: Amount of data to store (in gigabytes) if an rsyslog action takes time to process an incoming message (defaults to 1). Equivalent to the rsyslogd queue.maxdiskspace setting on the action (e.g. omhttp). It stores files in the directory specified by LOG_AGGREGATOR_MAX_DISK_USAGE_PATH. (integer) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_MAX_DISK_USAGE_PATH&#x60;: Location to persist logs that should be retried after an outage of the external log aggregator (defaults to /var/lib/awx). Equivalent to the rsyslogd queue.spoolDirectory setting. (string) * &#x60;LOG_AGGREGATOR_RSYSLOGD_DEBUG&#x60;: Enabled high verbosity debugging for rsyslogd.  Useful for debugging connection issues for external log aggregation. (boolean) * &#x60;API_400_ERROR_LOG_FORMAT&#x60;: The format of logged messages when an API 4XX error occurs, the following variables will be substituted:  status_code - The HTTP status code of the error user_name - The user name attempting to use the API url_path - The URL path to the API endpoint called remote_addr - The remote address seen for the user error - The error set by the api endpoint Variables need to be in the format {&amp;lt;variable name&amp;gt;}. (integer) * &#x60;IS_K8S&#x60;: Indicates whether the instance is part of a kubernetes-based deployment. (float) * &#x60;OPA_REQUEST_RETRIES&#x60;: The number of retry attempts for connecting to the OPA server. Default is 2. (integer) * &#x60;SESSION_COOKIE_AGE&#x60;: Number of seconds that a user is inactive before they will need to login again. (integer) * &#x60;SESSIONS_PER_USER&#x60;: Maximum number of simultaneous logged in sessions a user may have. To disable enter -1. (integer) * &#x60;DISABLE_LOCAL_AUTH&#x60;: Controls whether users are prevented from using the built-in authentication system.  (boolean) * &#x60;AUTH_BASIC_ENABLED&#x60;: Enable HTTP Basic Auth for the API Browser. (boolean) * &#x60;LOGIN_REDIRECT_OVERRIDE&#x60;: URL to which unauthorized users will be redirected to log in.  If blank, users will be sent to the login page. (string) * &#x60;ALLOW_METRICS_FOR_ANONYMOUS_USERS&#x60;: If true, anonymous users are allowed to poll metrics. (boolean) * &#x60;PENDO_TRACKING_STATE&#x60;: Enable or Disable User Analytics Tracking. (choice)     - &#x60;off&#x60;: Off     - &#x60;anonymous&#x60;: Anonymous     - &#x60;detailed&#x60;: Detailed * &#x60;CUSTOM_LOGIN_INFO&#x60;: If needed, you can add specific information (such as a legal notice or a disclaimer) to a text box in the login modal using this setting. Any content added must be in plain text or an HTML fragment, as other markup languages are not supported. (string) * &#x60;CUSTOM_LOGO&#x60;: To set up a custom logo, provide a file that you create. (string) * &#x60;MAX_UI_JOB_EVENTS&#x60;: Maximum number of job events for the UI to retrieve within a single request. (integer) * &#x60;UI_LIVE_UPDATES_ENABLED&#x60;: If disabled, the page will not refresh when events are received. Reloading the page will be required to get the latest details. (boolean) * &#x60;NAMED_URL_FORMATS&#x60;: Read-only list of key-value pairs that shows the standard format of all available named URLs. (integer, default&#x3D;&#x60;4000&#x60;) * &#x60;UI_LIVE_UPDATES_ENABLED&#x60;: If disabled, the page will not refresh when events are received. For a PATCH request, include only the fields that are being modified.    # Delete a Setting:  Make a DELETE request to this resource to delete this setting.
     * <p><b>200</b> - 
     * <p><b>400</b>
     * @param version  (required)
     * @param categorySlug  (required)
     * @param data  (optional)
     * @return SettingSingleton
     * @throws RestClientException if an error occurs while attempting to invoke the API
     */
    public SettingSingleton apiSettingsUpdate(String version, String categorySlug, Object data) throws RestClientException {
        return apiSettingsUpdateWithHttpInfo(version, categorySlug, data).getBody();
    }
